# BEHAVIOR.md — Conditional Logic Reference

Every behavioral rule in this prototype, stated as **WHEN condition → behavior**, with its source (`functionOrVariable`, ~approximate line in `src/components/AgentNextGenPage.tsx` unless another file is named). Line numbers drift as the file grows — grep the source name, not the number.

**Maintenance**: when you add or change conditional behavior, update the matching rule here (or add one) in the same commit. This file is the "what is supposed to happen" contract; the code is the "how."

---

## 1. Routing & top-level navigation

- WHEN hash is `#/agent` → Desk page (`AgentNextGenPage`); `#/agentworkspacepremium` → agent-workspace; `#/outboundengagement` → outbound; anything else (incl. empty) → `LoginPage` (`pageFromHash`, App.tsx ~L22)
- WHEN `navigate(page)` is called → `history.pushState`; browser `popstate` re-derives the page from the hash (`useHashRouter`, App.tsx ~L30)
- WHEN page === "agent" → Desk renders its own header/LeftNav; the shared Header/Sidebar/AiPanel shell is used only for outbound/agent-workspace (App.tsx ~L311)
- Desk content column is a 3-way exclusive switch: WHEN `showSettings` → Settings; ELSE WHEN `activeInteraction` → interaction record view; ELSE → dashboard (~L11993–12556)
- WHEN Settings rail item clicked → `setShowSettings(true)` + `switchActiveInteraction(null)`; Home → `switchActiveInteraction(null)` + `setShowSettings(false)` (~L11497)
- WHEN any `activeInteractionId` becomes truthy → `setShowSettings(false)` (~L8972)
- Home rail item `active` only WHEN `!hasActiveInteraction && !showSettings`; Settings active WHEN `showSettings` (`buildNavItems`, ~L767)
- WHEN switching branches → each remounts via `key` (`"settings"`, `interaction-${id}`, `"dashboard"`) so the entrance fade replays (~L12014)
- WHEN AgentProfile "Log Out" clicked → `onNavigate("login")` (~L11477)

## 2. Interactions & assignments — creation and merging

- WHEN CreateNew "Start Interaction" fires and no card exists for the contact → new card appended, `currentChannelId = newChannel.id`, `startedFresh: true`, `recordId = contact.subtitle ?? generateCaseId()` (`handleStartCall`, ~L9726)
- WHEN the same contact already has a card AND the same channel type+address exists → that channel row is REPLACED in place (timer restarts, its `channelStatuses` entry cleared); `liveMessages` deliberately kept (~L9759)
- WHEN the same contact, same channel type, but a DIFFERENT address → a NEW channel row is added alongside (two SMS threads on different numbers are separate conversations) (~L9752)
- WHEN the channel existed with `channelStatuses[id] === "Closed"` → treated as a reopen: `reopenedSessions` entry pushed with `messagesBeforeReopen` so prior messages stay visible (dimmed) under their own session (`isReopenOfClosedChannel`, ~L9684)
- WHEN any channel is started/restarted → it always becomes `currentChannelId` (~L9765)
- WHEN quick dial is used → card id `quickdial:<number>`, single literal `"voice"` channel; re-dial wholesale-replaces `channels` and resets `channelStatuses`/`liveMessages` (`handleQuickDial`, ~L9858)
- WHEN Redial on a Contact History row → id `entry.customerId ?? redial:<id>`; same wholesale replace; carries the real customer name; synthetic ids get no "+" Add Channel button (`handleRedial`, ~L9924)
- WHEN a Contact History ROW (not Redial) is clicked → reopens that past interaction: single channel of `entry.channelType`, `closed = entry.closed`, `channelStatuses = {id: entry.statusLabel}`, `liveMessages` cleared, `startedFresh` unset (`handleReopenContactHistoryEntry`, ~L9987)
- WHEN a channel/address is currently open for a contact → that exact address is disabled in CreateNew's channel/address pickers; Voice is blocked by TYPE (no per-address concept); a "Closed" channel no longer counts as open and can be reselected to reopen (`outboundConfig`, ~L10297)
- WHEN a launched interaction is genuinely new → Customer Information always starts CLOSED (`setSidePanelOpen(false)`, hardcoded — the earlier "opens per the agent's last choice" behavior, `lastSidePanelOpenChoice`, was removed per explicit follow-up request, see §153); an existing card's panel state is left as the agent left it (~L9798)
- `messageCount` is `undefined` for voice, `0` for digital channels (~L9740s)

## 3. Channel selection & sync

- WHEN a nav-card channel row OR a header ChannelTab is clicked → both call `handleChannelSelect(interactionId, channelKey)`, writing `interaction.currentChannelId` — that single field drives the card highlight, the active tab, and the transcript's `liveMessages[activeChannelKey]` (~L10127, L11776, L12316)
- WHEN nothing is explicitly current → the LAST channel in `channels` is the fallback everywhere (card, tab bar, `handleSendMessage`) (~L11707)
- WHEN a card (not a channel row) is clicked → `switchActiveInteraction(id)` plus a direct `setPanelFullScreen(false)` (clicking the already-active card changes no state, so the reset effect wouldn't fire) (~L11734)
- WHEN the currently-selected channel is dismissed → "current" passes to the new last remaining channel (`handleDismissChannel`, ~L10119)

## 4. Awaiting-response & SLA

- WHEN the agent sends a message → that channel's `awaitingResponse` set false immediately (`handleSendMessage`, ~L10210)
- WHEN the simulated customer reply lands (2500ms later) → `awaitingResponse: true` and `lastCustomerMessageTick = clockTick` on the channel captured AT SEND TIME (`channelKeyAtSend`), so switching tabs mid-wait can't misattribute the reply (~L10164)
- WHEN any open channel on a card awaits → card-level `awaitingResponse` true; severity uses the OLDEST wait among awaiting channels (`cardAwaitingChannels`, ~L11696)
- WHEN a channel's status is "Closed" → its awaiting state/severity forced off (row and tab) (`effectiveAwaitingResponse`, ~L11591)
- Severity: wait ≥ `AWAITING_CRITICAL_SECONDS` → critical (red); ≥ warning threshold → warning (amber); else success (green = just responded) (`getAwaitingSeverity`, ~L740)
- WHEN active-channel severity is warning/critical AND neither interaction nor channel is closed → SLA InlineNotification ("nearing SLA breach" / "breached SLA time") (~L12474)

## 5. Timers

- Shared 1s `clockTick` interval drives all interaction timers (~L8922); the agent-status timer is a separate clock
- Channel `startTick` = tick at creation → per-channel elapsed = time since opened; WHEN awaiting → reads `clockTick − lastCustomerMessageTick` instead (~L11567)
- Card elapsed = awaiting-time if any channel awaits, else time since the OLDEST open channel started (~L11685)
- WHEN every channel on a card is closed → elapsed renders empty (counter disappears rather than freezing) (~L11740)
- WHEN agent status changes → status timer resets to 0 (`handleStatusChange`, ~L9642)

## 6. Interaction switching & state persistence

- WHEN `activeInteractionId` or `showSettings` changes → shared panel fullscreen exits (~L9115)
- WHEN switching away from an assignment → its Customer Information `{open, fullScreen}` is snapshotted per-id (`sidePanelStateByAssignmentId`) and restored on return; a never-seen id gets `fullScreen: false` and `sidePanelOpen` left to the call site (`switchActiveInteraction`, ~L9520)
- WHEN a card is dismissed → its snapshot entry is deleted (~L10084)
- WHEN switching to a DIFFERENT interaction → content column (`key=interaction-${id}`) and side-panel wrapper (`key=side-panel-${id}`) remount: panel tab resets to Overview, `selectedHistoryIndex` → null. Navigating Home/Settings and back to the SAME interaction preserves them (~L12068, L11838, L7511)
- `liveMessages` persist across switches (stored on `ActiveInteraction`, not component state) (~L588)
- `interactions`, `deskTabOrder`, `activeDeskTab`, notifications persist across Home/Settings navigation (component stays mounted)

## 7. Closing, outcomes & read-only

- WHEN "Unassign & Dismiss" on a card with ONE open channel → logs a Contact History entry, success toast, removes the interaction, deletes its panel snapshot, hands active to `remaining[0]` (`handleDismissInteraction`, ~L10050)
- WHEN more than one channel is open → `handleDismissChannel` removes only that channel (matched by `id ?? type`, never type alone) and does NOT log history (the interaction isn't over) (~L10095)
- Dismissal logging: `statusLabel` = primary channel's last status, default "Resolved"; duration = `clockTick − earliestStart`; `closed` deliberately unset so the row reopens reply-able (~L2076)
- Status popover: non-"Closed" statuses apply immediately; "Closed" swaps to a "Close Contact?" confirm body first; reopening the popover always starts on the list view, never a stale confirm (`selectSessionStatus`/`handleStatusMenuOpenChange`, ~L4982–5026)
- Outcome popover: 3 entry points (ChannelRow kebab, transcript separator, ChannelTab kebab) share one draft (`outcomeDraftKey = interactionId:channelKey` + `outcomeDraftSource`); the draft resets on open; Approve & Save / Cancel only close — except Resolution, which writes through to `channelStatuses` (~L8699)
- WHEN `activeInteraction.closed` → full read-only: banner "You are viewing a closed interaction.", composer hidden, channel kebabs/menus hidden, transcript dimmed (~L12433)
- WHEN only the active channel is "Closed" → "This channel is closed. Reopen it to continue" banner; that channel's composer hides; other channels stay usable (~L12448)
- WHEN channel type is email or voice → composer hidden regardless (~L12546)

## 8. Contact History (dashboard) vs Customer History (panel tab)

- Contact History "Today" starts EMPTY; it fills only as the agent dismisses assignments (`dismissedContactHistory`) (~L1795)
- Ranges are cumulative supersets: last48h adds 5 hand-authored rows; last72h adds 5 more built from `CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMERS` (`buildContactHistoryByRange`, ~L2156; default `"today"`)
- Search filters name/description/caseId/channelLabel, case-insensitive (~L2250)
- Customer History (panel "Interactions" tab): 8 entries DETERMINISTICALLY SYNTHESIZED per customer, most-recent-first, stepping back 2–47h per entry (`buildCustomerHistoryEntries`, ~L6452); no `recordId` → tab renders nothing (~L7482)
- Its date filter defaults to `"last30"` because synthesized entries reach ~16 days back — "Last 7 days" would hide most history; Clear-all returns to `last30` (~L6578)
- Detail-panel chevrons walk the FULL UNFILTERED entries array (filtered rows keep their original index) (~L6674, `handleHistoryNav` ~L7528)
- WHEN the session detail panel opens → it lands on the **Conversation** tab (not Details); the active tab is NOT reset when chevroning between sessions — an agent scanning back through sessions keeps whichever tab they chose (`CustomerHistorySessionDetailPanel`, `useState(indexOf("Conversation"))`, no reset effect)
- WHEN the Conversation tab renders → a one-line outcome header (`statusLabel · agentName · timestampDisplay`) sits above the thread, so "how did that end?" lookups can finish without reading messages
- WHEN Overview's "Latest Interaction" accordion renders in the REAL side panel (not the hover preview) and history entries exist → an "Open Conversation" ghost button opens the newest past session's conversation as a TAB in the interaction space (record header) — full reading width, not inside the panel (`onOpenConversation` → `historyConversationTab`)
- WHEN the history conversation tab is open → it renders after the channel tabs as a plain `Tab` (History icon + session timestamp, NOT a `ChannelTab` — no awaiting state/address/outcome); its content REPLACES the live transcript/composer (read-only archived session, no composer); only one such tab exists at a time — opening another conversation replaces it
- WHEN a channel tab is clicked while the history tab is active → the history tab DEACTIVATES but stays in the strip (channel tabs' `active` is suppressed while the history view is active); clicking the history tab re-activates it; its kebab's "Close Tab" removes it
- WHEN switching to a DIFFERENT interaction (`switchActiveInteraction`) → the history conversation tab is cleared
- WHEN a new/different interaction becomes active → panel remounts: Overview first, no lingering history selection (~L11866)

## 9. Header app panel (single shared slot)

- WHEN a panel icon is clicked while the panel is closed → variant forced `"docked"`, panel opens (first open is never a float) (`handlePanelButtonClick`, ~L10824)
- WHEN open and the clicked key === `activePanelKey` → panel closes; WHEN a different key → only content swaps (no resize/reposition/re-animate) (~L10825)
- Only ONE shared panel exists (`activePanelKey`); there is no multi-panel z-order competition (~L10503)
- Open: float anchor computed once, height = `min(windowHeight − containerTop − 8, 860)`, recomputed on resize (`computePanelHeight`, ~L9625)
- Close: 150ms closing state, fullscreen forced off (~L10444)
- Dock: current rect captured for later undock; width resets unconditionally to `SHARED_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH` (~L10522)
- Float: `position: fixed; zIndex: 40` — deliberately below header menus' `z-[9999]`; float gets shadow + 80%-opacity blur background, docked is opaque full-height (~L10583, L11089)
- Fullscreen: overlay `absolute … z-[9]` inside the content container (LeftNav + AppHeader stay visible; above Customer Info `z-[5]`, below LeftNav toggle `z-10`); both Draggable wrappers unmount; enter = double-rAF fade/scale, exit = 180ms delay (~L11195, L10473)
- Fullscreen "Undock" → width 1024, computed height, variant float (~L10555); fullscreen X closes the panel entirely (~L11245)
- Resize bounds: width 280–1024, minHeight 400; width transition disabled while resizing (~L11068)

## 10. Header icon row & "View All Apps"

- WHEN the gap between AppName and the icon row < 16px → hide the farthest-left pinned icon; WHEN gap > 84px → reveal one (hysteresis prevents flapping) (~L11012)
- `showHeaderIcon = pinned && !responsivelyHidden` (~L11057)
- Defaults: `notif`, `conversations`, `search` pinned; others unpinned (~L9198)
- `customers`/`accounts`/`tickets`/`wem` are hidden from the kebab menu (`HIDDEN_FROM_APPS_MENU`) — unpinning them makes them unreachable (~L10899)
- One `useColumnReorder(panelOrder)` instance drives BOTH the header icons and the kebab rows (drag either surface, same order) (~L10878)
- WHEN the notifications icon isn't shown → the unread count moves onto the kebab badge (never shown twice) (~L11459)
- Active key gets the blue selected treatment on both surfaces (~L10910); kebab rows use `closeOnSelect: false` (~L10911)
- WHEN `windowWidth < 760` → compact AppName + AppMenu header (~L9596)

## 11. Left nav

- WHEN `bodyContainerWidth < 768` (ResizeObserver on the nav+content row, NOT the viewport) → `overlay={true}` hover-to-open mode; if expanded, auto-collapses (~L9157, L9599)
- Starting/reopening an assignment does NOT auto-expand the rail (removed on purpose) (~L9647)
- WHEN collapsed and a card has exactly ONE open channel → `CollapsedChannelBadge` overlays the tile corner (skipped at 2+ channels — the card's own count badge takes over); warning/critical severity replaces the channel accent with solid warning/destructive + alert glyph (~L11791; CollapsedChannelBadge.tsx ~L160)
- In overlay mode, `expanded={navOpen}` is passed directly to `CreateNew`/caption/cards (LeftNav's `injectExpanded` can't reach wrapped children) (~L9814)
- Non-Desk shell (App.tsx): viewport < 1280 → sidebar overlay + auto-collapse, persisting `lyra_sidebar_open=false` (App.tsx ~L224)

## 12. Customer Information panel & hover preview

- The person-icon toggle renders only while the panel is closed AND an interaction is active; its grid track animates in 100ms after the panel finishes closing; hide edge is instant (~L9414, L12148)
- WHEN content width < 768 → panel forced UNPINNED (floats as overlay); `sidePanelOpen` is not force-changed (~L9341)
- WHEN content width ≤ 425 and open → fullscreen forced ON (one-way: widening doesn't force it off) (~L9359)
- WHEN content width ≤ 350 → fullscreen toggle hidden; crossing back above 350 restores the button AND exits fullscreen (~L9379)
- Hover: pinned → hover does nothing either direction; unpinned → hover-in opens instantly, hover-out closes after 300ms; while resizing, mouse-leave is unwired (drag past the edge can't close it) (~L9430)
- Header close: closes + exits fullscreen; pin state untouched (`handleSidePanelClose`, ~L9446) — no longer records a "last choice" (see §153)
- Icon toggle click: always toggles (never a no-op), closes/cancels the hover preview (`handleSidePanelIconToggle`, ~L9550)
- Hover preview popover: opens instantly on trigger hover/focus, closes 150ms after leaving trigger AND content (gap-crossing safe); `onOpenAutoFocus`/`onCloseAutoFocus` prevented (the flash-loop guard); forced closed when the active interaction changes (~L9571, L12220, L9582)
- Panel remounts per assignment (`key=side-panel-${id}`) → fade-in instead of width-slide; wrapper carries explicit `z-[5]` because `animate-in` creates a stacking context that would otherwise lose to the content column (~L11912, L11880)
- Width: starts 340, max 425; pinned state and width are GLOBAL across assignments (only open/fullscreen snapshot per-assignment) (~L9295, L9520)
- WHEN the Copilot tab would otherwise show (all 3 tiers, both the docked panel and the hover preview) → it's hidden entirely — `copilotAvailable` is hardcoded `false` (`CustomerInformationSidePanel`/`CustomerInfoHoverPreview`, agent-next-gen-customer-info-panel.tsx), superseding the "hidden until the customer actually responds" gating it used to have. Per later explicit follow-up request, the auto-jump-to-Copilot-on-customer-reply behavior (`copilotFocusSignal`/`copilotFocusRequest`, formerly wired from each page's `handleSendMessage` simulated-reply timeout into the docked panel) was reported as annoying and has been removed outright, not just hidden — no state, effect, or prop for it remains in any of the 3 page files or the shared panel component. "Copilot" stays listed in `CUSTOMER_PANEL_TABS`/`AGENT_WORKSPACE_CUSTOMER_PANEL_TABS`, so re-enabling later is a one-line revert of `copilotAvailable`'s hardcoded value (the auto-jump wiring would need to be re-added separately if wanted back).

## 13. Interior panels

- Customer History session detail: opens on card click (`selectedHistoryIndex`); `z-[3]` — above the list, below Customer Info's `z-[5]` (both would otherwise collide at `z-[5]`) (~L6983, L7905)
- Below InteriorPanel's ~1024px internal threshold it overlays the list; at ≥1024 it docks inline side-by-side (~L7869)
- Chevrons: `hasPrevious/hasNext` bound to the entries array; `activeTab` resets on every `entry.id` change (~L7910, L6978)
- Customers table: row click opens `CustomerRowInfoPanel`; clicking the already-open row CLOSES it; chevrons walk the filtered+sorted rows; tab resets keyed on `contactNumber`; measured width < 480 collapses header actions into a kebab (~L12705, L8852, L8373)
- Dashboard: queue-card click opens the ONE shared right InteriorPanel (`selectedQueueId` takes priority over the Case Details content); close clears both flags (~L12828)

## 14. Responsive thresholds (consolidated)

| Threshold | Measured on | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| < 1280px | viewport (App.tsx shell) | sidebar overlay + auto-collapse (non-Desk pages) |
| < 768px | nav+content row width | LeftNav overlay mode; nav auto-collapse; enables combined-panel mode |
| combined mode | `isNavNarrow && panelOpen && docked` | docked panel merges with main region into ONE shared container, switched via a full-width `ToggleGroup` segmented control (was `TabList`/`Tab` — per explicit follow-up request "use toggle buttons instead of tabs for the combined panels," all 3 tiers: a two-item single-select switch reads as "which surface is showing," distinct from any real `TabList` — e.g. the record header's own channel tabs — that might render directly underneath it once the main region is showing; per a further explicit follow-up request, `ToggleGroup` gained its own new `fullWidth` prop — mirroring `TabList`'s own — so this switch spans the row edge-to-edge with each side an equal-width column, instead of `ToggleGroup`'s normal compact, content-width sizing); icon click switches which side is active; undock exits combined mode (~L10811) |
| < 768px | content container | Customer Info forced unpinned overlay |
| ≤ 425px | content container | Customer Info forced fullscreen (one-way) |
| ≤ 350px | content container | fullscreen toggle hidden; crossing up exits fullscreen |
| < 760px | viewport | compact AppName + AppMenu header |
| < 480px | CustomerRowInfoPanel width | header actions collapse to kebab |
| ~1024px | InteriorPanel internal | overlay vs inline docking |
| < 400px | InteriorPanel internal | auto full-screen (was 768px) |
| < 16px / > 84px | AppName↔icons gap | hide / reveal header icons (hysteresis) |
| < 400px (CSS) | `lyra-transcript-wrap` container | transcript avatar collapse |
| < 480px (CSS) | filter-chip container | filter chips go icon-only |
| 768px | SchedulePanel toolbar | one-row vs two-row toolbar |

## 15. Notifications

- Click behavior by type: `new-case`/`escalation` → open/create assignment + mark read; `new-agent-chat` → switch shared panel to Agent Chat + mark read (no assignment); `new-chat`/`missed-call` → mark read only (~L10682)
- Assignment resolution: customer id = `CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMERS[Number(id) % length].id` (deterministic — re-clicks hit the same card); channel = `NOTIFICATION_CHANNEL[id] ?? "email"`; existing card → channels wholesale-replaced, name preserved; new card → `customerName = subtitle`, `startedFresh` NOT set (full mock history shows) (~L10608)
- Titles derive from channel: `New ${noun}` / `Escalation - ${noun}`; sms/whatsapp/chat all read "Chat", voice "Call" (~L1061)
- Unread badge = count of `!read`; moves to the kebab when the bell icon is hidden; Mark-all sets read, Clear-all empties, per-row dismiss filters (~L10936, L10669)

## 16. Agent status & welcome

- Initial: `unavailable` + welcome modal (not dismissible by backdrop or Escape); "Go Available"/"Start Unavailable" both set status and reset the timer (~L8977, L10418, L13086)
- Status change always resets the status timer; ticks 1s, formats HH:MM:SS (~L9642)
- Dark mode: initialized from `<html data-theme>`; toggle writes it back (~L8981)
- Welcome modal backdrop uses `color-mix()` on the shell token so it follows the theme (~L13081)
- `hideConnectedApps` omits that status-menu row; `onHelpClick` adds a Help row (~L11483)

## 17. CreateNew / New Outbound

- Default group `"all"` (favorites-style: idle shows starred, typing widens); `dialpad` group filtered out via `HIDDEN_OUTBOUND_GROUP_IDS` (quick-dial UI unreachable; handlers still wired) (~L290)
- `hideContactList: true` — group dropdown + search only (~L346)
- Trigger click → search input focused (rAF + 50ms) with `autocomplete="off"` (~L9827)
- Customers-tab channel popover: Start blocked unless skill set AND an unused address remains; channels with no remaining address disabled; each open resets channel to default and clears skill (~L2628)
- `OutboundAddButton` is self-contained — `useOutboundAddButton` must receive `{...outboundConfig, onStartCall: handleStartCall}` or Start only console-logs (~L10393)
- Escape: steps back one level in CreateNew; closes the composer quick-reply menu; the welcome modal ignores it

## 18. Screen Pop

- Default `"salesforce"`; salesforce/zendesk render `MockLoginCard` variants; all other apps → "Nothing here yet."; the app Select lives in `headerContent` so it never scrolls (~L9176, L10729)

## 19. Dashboard

- Contact-history date filter: Today (default) / Last 48 Hours / Last 72 Hours — cumulative ranges (see §8)
- Performance cards' DateFilterChip: Today (default) / Yesterday / Last 7 days / Custom → Custom reveals a DateRangePicker (~L1495)
- Customers list stays MOUNTED when its tab is inactive (`absolute inset-0 opacity-0 inert`) — state survives tab switches; accounts/tickets/wem show "Coming soon" (~L12686)

## 20. Composer

- Send enabled only when trimmed message is non-empty (~L5631)
- Hidden when: interaction closed, active channel "Closed", or channel type email/voice (~L12546)
- Send → append to `liveMessages[channelKeyAtSend]`, clear that channel's awaiting; canned customer reply lands 2500ms later on the SAME captured key with `awaitingResponse: true` (~L10151)
- `/`-prefixed token opens the quick-reply menu; arrows/Enter/Escape handled in `handleComposerKeyDown` (~L5628)

## 21. Transcript

- WHEN the transcript is scrolled to the very bottom → the LAST session's own content wrapper (agent-next-gen-transcript.tsx) gets an extra `pb-9` (36px) on top of its message blocks' own `py-4` — per explicit follow-up request ("increase the padding-bottom of the conversation container... so the bottom comments are not covered by the fade"): the composer's own soft top fade overlay is `-top-8 h-8` (32px), which otherwise painted over the last message's own text once scrolled all the way down. 36px (a touch more than the fade's 32px) clears it. Scoped to `session.id === lastSessionId` only — every older session already has real content below it in the scroll (its own separator, or the next session), so this extra clearance is only needed once, at the true end of the document; whichever sub-block actually renders last for that session (canned `messages`, the Voice/Email placeholder, or live messages) is covered either way, since the padding lives on their shared parent wrapper, not on any one of them individually.
- Session separators are `sticky top-0 z-[1]`; a separator's session block must contain its own messages (incl. live ones) or a fresh session has ~zero height and unsticks; a `container-type` ancestor between separator and scroller breaks stickiness (~L4183, L5308)
- Sessions expand independently (`openSessionIds` is a Set) (~L4937)
- Only the CURRENT session (`lastSessionId`) has a working status popover routed to the interaction; older sessions use local overrides and get no Outcome popover / dismiss button (~L5000, L4147)
- Transcript dismiss: >1 channel removes just the channel; otherwise the whole interaction (~L12527)
- Voice/email with no messages → "Coming Soon" placeholder (~L5359)
- Per-message actions appear on hover/focus-within, forced visible while that message's tag picker is open; tag picker is multi-select and deliberately stays open; one open picker at a time (~L3866, L5042)
- Mount scrolls to bottom ONCE (layout effect, empty deps); "N new" chip appears only when scrolled away from bottom (~L5097)
- Closed interaction or closed channel → transcript `dimmed` (~L12498)

## 22. Record header status, toasts & new-outbound-thread lockdown (all 3 tiers unless noted)

- WHEN the interaction has no webchat (`chat`) thread open on it AT ALL → the record-header "Active" badge never renders, even while the interaction/channel is otherwise open — there's no reliable "customer is online" signal for SMS/WhatsApp/Voice/Email (`badge={activeInteraction.closed ? "Closed" : hasOpenChatThread ? "Active" : undefined}`, all 3 `PageHeader` call sites; `badgeColor` logic unchanged). Originally this read `activeChannelType === "chat"` (the currently-SELECTED tab); per explicit follow-up request ("leave the active badge visible if the agent switches between chat/other channels") it now reads `hasOpenChatThread = !!activeInteraction?.threads.some((c) => c.type === "chat")` instead, so switching tabs away from an open chat thread to view another channel on the same interaction no longer hides the badge — it only disappears once that chat thread itself is closed/removed.
- WHEN on Agent Workspace 2.0 specifically (2.0 only) → the thread page-header subtitle reads `{channel icon} {channel type} | {date/time}` instead of the plain customer ID every other tier still shows (`resolveActiveChannelDateTimeLabel`/`formatCompactDateTime`, `AgentNextGenPage.tsx`; resolution order: latest `reopenedContacts` entry → `isFreshLaunch` branch (see next bullet) → that channel type's last fixed mock session from `TRANSCRIPT_SESSIONS`/`_VOICE`/`_EMAIL`)
- WHEN a fresh (`isFreshLaunch`) chat/SMS/WhatsApp thread hasn't had the agent send its first live message yet → the subtitle's date/time portion literally reads `"Draft"` instead of a timestamp (`threadLaunchTimestamps` has no entry yet for this `${interactionId}:${channelKey}`); WHEN `handleSendMessage` fires that channel's first-ever send → the real send `Date` is captured ONCE into `threadLaunchTimestamps` and the subtitle switches to that frozen date/time for good (never re-captured, never keeps ticking to "now"). Voice/Email are unaffected — they still resolve straight to `formatCompactDateTime(new Date())` the whole time `isFreshLaunch` is true, since neither has a real send/dial action in this prototype to gate "Draft" on (composer is hidden for both, §20)
- WHEN a channel's status has never been explicitly assigned (via the status pill or Outcome popover) → every UI read of that status falls back to `"Open"`, not `"Resolved"` (~12 sites: LeftNav card Outcome, 2.0 header Outcome popover + `ChannelStatusTag`, record-header tab tooltip + kebab Outcome, `InteractionTranscript`'s current-session `outcome.resolution`) — EXCEPT `agent-next-gen-contact-history.tsx`'s `buildDismissedContactHistoryEntry`, which still defaults an ALREADY-dismissed historical entry's `statusLabel` to `"Resolved"` (that one only ever describes a concluded conversation, so the old default is still correct there)
- WHEN a session-status changes (pill or Outcome popover Resolution field) → no toast fires anymore, in any of the 3 tiers (`handleInteractionStatusChange` — `addToast` call removed; the underlying status write is unchanged)
- WHEN `interaction.startedFresh` is true AND the active/that channel has no `lastCustomerMessageTick` yet (`const isNewOutboundThread`/`activeChannelIsNewOutboundThread`, i.e. a brand-new, agent-initiated OUTBOUND thread the customer hasn't replied to) → Consult/Transfer, Outcome, and Unassign & Dismiss are hidden across 4 surfaces and replaced with a plain close ("×") affordance: the LeftNav card kebab (`removable: isClosed || isNewOutboundThread ? false : undefined`), the record-header `ChannelTab` kebab (`showMenu` additionally requires `!isNewOutboundThread`), the session row (`TranscriptSessionSeparator`'s new `isNewThread` prop — keeps the status chip, swaps the red Unassign & Dismiss button for a plain ghost `X`), and (2.0 only) the header's own icon cluster (Consult/Transfer, Outcome, Send/Download Transcript, Translate Messages all hidden; status chip stays; Unassign & Dismiss becomes a plain close `ActionIconButton`) — this 2.0-header collapse is this file's own extrapolation of the other two tiers' tab-kebab treatment, not a surface the original request named explicitly
- WHEN the customer actually replies (`lastCustomerMessageTick` gets set) → the above lockdown lifts automatically on its own, even though `startedFresh` itself never resets — same "still genuinely fresh vs. already has real activity" split `copilotAvailable` already uses for the same flag
- WHEN a thread was opened by the CUSTOMER instead of the agent (notification click, Interactions-table row click — neither of those handlers ever sets `startedFresh`) → none of the above lockdown ever applies, regardless of message history; it keeps the full normal action set like any other live thread
- Icon sizing: every icon in the 2.0 header's action cluster, and `UserX` in the session row's own cluster, renders at `h-4 w-4` (16px) — matches `ConsultTransferIcon`'s fixed size; the smaller `h-3.5 w-3.5` these used to be reads as visibly mismatched sitting next to it
- "Unassign & Dismiss" is an icon-only outline-critical `Button` (`UserX`, no visible label — per later explicit follow-up request, "takes up less space") everywhere it renders: all 3 tiers' header clusters (`size="icon-md"`, 32px) and the shared session row (`TranscriptSessionSeparator`, `size="icon-sm"`, 24px, matching that row's other icon buttons) — composed from `Button`'s `outline` variant plus critical-status token overrides (no named `outline`+`critical` variant exists on `Button` itself), with the former label text moved into `title`, which `Button` auto-wraps in a Tooltip + sets as `aria-label` for any `icon-*` size (button.tsx) — no hand-wrapped `Tooltip` needed. In the 2.0 header it sits at the cluster's FAR RIGHT, past the status chip, behind a vertical `Separator`. On Premium/Advanced its position is now DIFFERENT from 2.0 — see the bullet below.
- WHEN an interaction on Premium (`AgentWorkspace2WithDeskPage.tsx`) or Advanced (`AgentWorkspaceAdvancedPage.tsx`) is NOT backed by a real `CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMERS` directory record (`activeInteractionIsRealCustomer = CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMERS.some((c) => c.id === activeInteraction.id)`, `false` for `quickdial:`/`adhoc:`/synthetic `redial:`/hand-authored Contact History ids) → that tier now gets 2.0's own no-tab-row treatment: the channel `TabList` row is hidden entirely (`showChannelTabRow = !activeInteraction || activeInteractionIsRealCustomer`) and the record header's own icon-button cluster (Consult/Transfer, Outcome, Send/Download Transcript, Translate Messages, status chip, Unassign & Dismiss — same "new outbound thread" close-only collapse as 2.0, §22 above) renders in the tab row's place instead, AND the docked Customer Information panel is restricted to ONLY the Detail tab (`tabs={activeInteractionIsRealCustomer ? <tier's normal set> : (["Detail"] as const)}` — Premium's normal set is the full `CUSTOMER_PANEL_TABS`, Advanced's is the already-reduced `AGENT_WORKSPACE_CUSTOMER_PANEL_TABS`). This is keyed on customer identity alone, for the interaction's WHOLE LIFETIME (not just while `startedFresh`) — an old reopened synthetic Contact History entry gets it too. An interaction WITH a real customer record keeps both tiers' full tab row + full tab set exactly as before, unchanged.
- Follow-up to the bullet above (screenshot report: duplicate status chip/close/Unassign & Dismiss visible in both the header cluster AND the session row at once): on Premium/Advanced, `InteractionTranscript`'s `showSessionActionCluster` prop is now wired to that same `showChannelTabRow` value (`showSessionActionCluster={showChannelTabRow}`, same call site as `isNewThread`) instead of being left unset (previously always defaulted to `true`) — so the session row's OWN copy of the cluster (status chip, Consult/Transfer, Outcome, Unassign & Dismiss/close — `TranscriptSessionSeparator`'s `showActionCluster`, agent-next-gen-transcript.tsx) hides itself the moment the header cluster takes over for a non-real-customer interaction, the same substitution 2.0 makes permanently via its own hardcoded `showSessionActionCluster = false`. A real-customer interaction keeps the session row's cluster exactly as before (both tiers never touched this prop until now).
- Per explicit follow-up request, on Premium/Advanced ONLY (2.0's own position, described above, is unchanged): the header cluster's Unassign & Dismiss button — and its "new outbound thread" Close (`×`) alternate, moved together as one unit per an explicit mid-turn clarification ("close button should also be positioned far right") — now renders AFTER the Customer Information toggle `Button`/`Popover`, at the very end of the header's `actions` fragment, instead of immediately after `ChannelStatusTag`/behind the vertical `Separator` (that bare `Separator` still renders in its old spot, just with nothing after it anymore). Only affects `!showChannelTabRow` interactions (the header cluster is the only surface this button/close-alternate renders on for those two tiers); 2.0's own header cluster position is untouched.
- Per the same explicit follow-up request ("update the subhead of the unknown contacts to have the same icon + thread type + Draft/date pattern as 2.0"): on Premium/Advanced, an unknown-contact interaction's (`!showChannelTabRow`) `PageHeader` `subtitle` now renders 2.0's own "{channel icon} {Channel label} | {date/time, or the literal `Draft` pre-launch}" pattern instead of the tier's plain customer-ID text — built from the SAME duplicated-verbatim helpers 2.0 uses (`formatCompactDateTime`, `resolveActiveChannelDateTimeLabel`, `threadLaunchTimestamps` capture-on-first-send in `handleSendMessage`, `CHANNEL_TYPE_META` for the icon/label) per this codebase's "no shared sync" convention — see §21's equivalent 2.0-only bullets above for the full priority-order rationale (reopen date > Draft/frozen-send-time for fresh text channels > `new Date()` for fresh voice/email > last transcript session's date). A real-customer interaction (`showChannelTabRow` true) keeps each tier's existing plain customer-ID subtitle unchanged — this port only ever swaps in the new pattern for the unknown-contact case.
- Per explicit request, Premium/Advanced ONLY (this whole flow can't apply to 2.0 — its Customer Information panel is hidden entirely for its interaction view, §12 above): when the docked Customer Information panel is showing an unknown-contact interaction (`!activeInteractionIsRealCustomer`), it no longer just renders a blank Detail-tab form — it runs a simulated "does this look like anyone in the customer directory" check first (`findPossibleCustomerMatches`, agent-next-gen-customer-info-panel.tsx — there's no real matching backend, so this is a DETERMINISTIC hash of the channel's own dialed/typed identifier: ~30% of identifiers come back with zero matches, the rest return 1-8 possible matches pulled from `CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMERS` + this session's own `createdCustomerRecords`) and shows the result instead: a "Customer Information" header reading "0 Matches" (empty state: "No Customers Match This Interaction") or "N Possible Matches" (a list of candidate customers, each row hover-revealing a "Link To Record" button in place of its chevron), plus a "Search Customers" `SearchInput` that switches the same list over to real substring search results (`filterCustomersByQuery`) the moment anything's typed, and a full-width "Create New Customer" button pinned to the footer. Clicking "Create New Customer" swaps the panel over to a "← Create New Customer" header (back arrow returns to the search step without discarding anything) with the SAME Detail-tab form/draft this panel already showed for the interaction (`CustomerDetailTabContent` + the caller's own `recordDraft` — no separate draft needed) and a Cancel/Save footer. Both resolutions promote the interaction to a real customer by reassigning its `id` (the left-nav slot key `activeInteractionIsRealCustomer` checks) — `handleLinkCustomerRecord` (picking an existing match) ALSO reassigns `customerId`/`customerName` to the matched record's own (resetting `recordDraft` to that record's fresh synthesized data); `handleSaveNewCustomer` (the create form) mints a brand-new `CreateNewCustomerRecord` from the typed first/last name into `createdCustomerRecords` (an in-memory, session-only "directory" — there's no real backend to persist into the actual static `CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMERS` array) but deliberately KEEPS the interaction's existing case-number `customerId`, so `recordDraft` never resets and every typed field carries straight over into the resulting "known customer" Detail tab unchanged. Either way, the promotion cascades automatically through every other `activeInteractionIsRealCustomer`-gated surface (§21 above) — full channel tab row, full Customer Information tab set, plain customer-ID subtitle — the instant it resolves, with no separate wiring needed for any of those.
- Follow-up bug fix: the search step's "Create New Customer" button (bullet above) originally passed `variant="primary"` to `Button` — not a real variant this design system's `Button` recognizes (button.tsx's `cva` map only has `default`/`destructive`/`warning`/`success`/`outline`/`ghost`/`icon`), so it silently fell back to the base, unstyled classes (no fill color at all) instead of erroring, since `@nicecxone/lyra-ui`'s own type-checking is unavailable in this app's tsc setup (the whole package resolves to `any` — see CLAUDE.md). Fixed by omitting `variant` entirely — `Button`'s own default IS the filled/primary style, same as every other primary action in this panel (e.g. `CustomerRecordSaveFooter`'s own Save button).
- Follow-up per explicit request ("make sure the closed customer information hover panel displays the same information as the docked open panel"): `CustomerInfoHoverPreview` (the flyout shown on hovering the header's Customer Information toggle while the docked panel is closed) previously always passed the full/normal tab set (`CUSTOMER_PANEL_TABS` on Premium, `AGENT_WORKSPACE_CUSTOMER_PANEL_TABS` on Advanced) regardless of customer identity, and never received the new `matchState` prop (bullet above) at all — so for an unknown-contact interaction, hovering showed a totally different view (generic synthesized Overview data across every tab) than actually opening the docked panel would (the search/possible-matches/create-new-customer flow). Both call sites now pass the exact same `tabs={activeInteractionIsRealCustomer ? <tier's normal set> : (["Detail"] as const)}` conditional AND the exact same `matchState` object the docked `CustomerInformationSidePanel` call site builds, and `CustomerInfoHoverPreview` itself now has the identical header/body/footer `matchState` branching that component already had (search step's list/"Create New Customer" button, create step's Detail-tab-reusing form/Cancel-Save footer) — a shared `buildCustomerMatchSubhead` helper (agent-next-gen-customer-info-panel.tsx) computes the "N Possible Matches"/"N Matches"/"0 Matches" header text once, used by both components, so the two can never disagree on wording for the same interaction.
- Per explicit request ("update the close icon button on drafts... to be a trash can icon red and on hover say 'Delete Draft'"), across all 3 tiers: the close-only ("×") affordance that already replaced the kebab/Unassign & Dismiss for a brand-new outbound thread (`isNewOutboundThread`/`isNewThread`, §22 above) now renders as a red `Trash2` icon reading "Delete Draft" on hover, at every render site that treatment reaches — the LeftNav card's `ChannelRow` (lyra-ui, `InteractionChannel.removeVariant: "delete-draft" | "close"`, a new prop threaded alongside `removable` so an already-CLOSED channel/interaction's row still gets the plain neutral "×"/"Close" look, not the trash/red treatment), the record-header `ChannelTab`'s own kebab-replacement (lyra-ui, `ChannelTabProps.removeVariant`, same split, wired through to `Tab`'s own new `removeVariant` prop in tabs.tsx which swaps its hand-drawn "×" SVG for a `Trash2` icon and switches to `text-lyra-status-critical-strong` whenever it's `"delete-draft"`), the session row (`TranscriptSessionSeparator`'s `isNewThread` branch, agent-next-gen-transcript.tsx — shared across all 3 tiers, edited once), and each tier's own record-header icon-cluster substitute (the `activeChannelIsNewOutboundThread` ternary already isolated to the draft case, so this one just swaps `X`/"Close" for `Trash2`/"Delete Draft" directly, no new prop needed). An already-closed channel/interaction never gets the trash/red treatment anywhere — only a genuine, never-launched draft does, since `removeVariant`/the page-header ternary are both keyed on `isNewOutboundThread` specifically, not the broader "no kebab" condition `removable`/`showMenu` alone represent.
- Second half of the same request ("if a draft is deleted without an agent interacting with the customer then do not add it to the Contact History"): `handleDismissInteraction` (all 3 tiers) now checks `everSentAgentMessage = Object.values(dismissed.liveMessages ?? {}).some((messages) => messages.some((m) => m.sender === "agent"))` before doing ANY of its Contact History work (`saveCaseRecord`, `buildDismissedContactHistoryEntry`, `setDismissedContactHistory`) — a genuinely untouched draft (the agent never sent a single message on any of its channels) skips all three, so no phantom "Resolved" row appears for a conversation the customer never even saw; a dismissed assignment the agent actually worked (even just one message, even before any reply) still logs exactly as before. `handleDismissChannel` (dismissing one channel of an otherwise-still-open multi-channel card) never logged Contact History either way, so it only needed the same check for its OWN toast wording. `fireDismissToast` (all 3 tiers) gained a second `isDraftDelete` parameter (default `false`, every existing call site unaffected) — passed `!everSentAgentMessage` from both dismiss handlers — that swaps its message from "{name} {customerId} Successfully Dismissed" to "{name} Draft Deleted" for the genuine-draft case, so the confirmation toast matches the red trash "Delete Draft" affordance that triggered it.
- Per explicit request ("add these icons to the left of all of the items in the new outbound dropdown - match to the appropriate categories"), across all 3 tiers (shared config, `agent-next-gen-outbound-data.tsx`, one edit applies everywhere): the "New Outbound" picker's "Choose group" dropdown (lyra-ui's `CreateNew`, `Select`-backed) now shows a leading icon on each group option, matched by category — `Headphones` for "Agents", `Users` for "Teams", `Share2` for "Skills", `User` for "Customers" ("All" and the hidden "Dial Pad" group intentionally stay icon-less — neither was part of the requested set). Required two small lyra-ui additions since neither existed before: `Select`'s own `SelectOption` gained an optional `icon?: React.ReactNode` (select.tsx), rendered at a fixed `h-4 w-4` to the left of the label in BOTH the single-select `SelectPrimitive.Item` row and the multi-select popover's row button — purely additive, so every existing `Select` consumer that doesn't set an icon renders byte-for-byte as before; and `CreateNewOutboundGroup` gained the matching `icon?: React.ReactNode` (create-new.tsx), threaded straight into the "Choose group" `Select`'s own `options={...}` mapping (`icon: g.icon`). Icons aren't shown on the closed trigger's own selected-value text — Radix's `Select.Value` in single-select mode only accepts a placeholder node, not a live per-option icon — only inside the open dropdown's rows, which is exactly where the request pointed ("the items in the dropdown").
- Follow-up per explicit request ("i want these icons before the names of the people as well"): the same 4 category icons now also lead each individual CONTACT row within the picker (not just the group-dropdown option) — matters most for the "All"/favorites group, which mixes agents/teams/skills/customers in one list with no group heading between rows to tell them apart otherwise. `CreateNewContact` (the base type both the generic drill-down flow and `CreateNewOutboundContact` extend, create-new.tsx) gained an optional `categoryIcon?: React.ReactNode`, rendered in the shared `ContactRow` — distinct from `CreateNewCategory.icon` (that one already existed, labeling a CATEGORY list itself in the unrelated generic flow) and from the group-level `icon` added in the bullet above (that one labels the dropdown OPTION, not each resulting contact row). `agent-next-gen-outbound-data.tsx` now sets `categoryIcon` on every contact it builds — `OUTBOUND_AGENTS` (headset), `OUTBOUND_TEAMS` (people), `OUTBOUND_SKILLS` (share/network), `OUTBOUND_CUSTOMERS` and `contactHistoryOutboundContact` (single person, since every `ContactHistoryEntry` is a past customer contact) — via 4 shared bare-icon-factory functions (`agentCategoryIcon`/`teamCategoryIcon`/`skillCategoryIcon`/`customerCategoryIcon`, called fresh per row since one React element instance can't be reused across multiple rendered rows) that the group-dropdown's own `OUTBOUND_GROUPS.icon` values also call, so the group option and every contact under it can never drift to different icons for the same category. `OUTBOUND_TEAM_MEMBERS` (round-robins `OUTBOUND_AGENTS` per team) inherits the agent icon automatically, since it's filtered from `OUTBOUND_AGENTS` rather than rebuilt.
- Follow-up per explicit request ("I'd prefer you used the leading icon story instead of putting the icon inline with the name"): the bullet above originally rendered `categoryIcon` inline right before the name text inside `ContactRow`'s own title line — swapped instead to match lyra-ui's own `ListItem` "With leading icon" story (ListItem.stories.tsx) exactly: the icon now renders as its own flex item to the LEFT of the whole title/subtitle column (not squeezed onto the title's line), and `categoryIcon` itself is now a fully pre-built node — the icon wrapped in the same colored circle shell every example in that story hand-builds (`h-9 w-9 rounded-full {bg} flex items-center justify-center {text}`) — rather than a bare glyph, so `ContactRow` needs no sizing/color logic of its own for it (`CreateNewContact.categoryIcon`'s own doc comment, create-new.tsx, updated to match). `agent-next-gen-outbound-data.tsx` gained a `categoryLeadingIcon(icon, bgClassName, textClassName)` helper plus 4 per-category wrappers (`agentCategoryLeadingIcon`/`teamCategoryLeadingIcon`/`skillCategoryLeadingIcon`/`customerCategoryLeadingIcon`) built on top of the existing bare-icon functions — `categoryIcon` assignments on every contact now call these leading variants, while `OUTBOUND_GROUPS`'s own group-dropdown-option `icon` field is untouched (still the bare icon, matching `Select`'s own plain icon-row treatment, no circle). Colors are 4 existing design-system tokens, not invented: blue "active" for Agents, purple "accent" for Teams (the same token `OUTBOUND_TEAMS`'s own `t1` avatar already used), green "success" for Skills, neutral "surface shell" gray for Customers.
- WHEN an agent contact appears in the New Outbound picker → it's only reachable via Voice or Chat: `OUTBOUND_AGENTS`'s own `channels` field (`agent-next-gen-outbound-data.tsx`) is hardcoded to `["voice", "chat"]`, overriding whatever `CREATE_NEW_AGENTS` fixture data itself lists. A new `"chat"` entry was added to `OUTBOUND_CONFIG.channelOptions` (`MessageCircle` icon, matching `ChatChannelRow`/`CHANNEL_TYPE_META.chat`'s own icon in lyra-ui's `channel-row.tsx`) — the only consumer of that channel id, since no other outbound group's contacts list `"chat"` among their own channels.
- WHEN `selection.channel === "chat"` AND the picked contact is an agent (`OUTBOUND_AGENTS.some(a => a.id === selection.contact.id)`) → `handleStartCall` (all 3 page files) special-cases this at the very top of the function: calls `handlePanelButtonClick("conversations")()` (the existing Agent Chat docked panel — same generic-open pattern the "New Agent Chat" notification handler already uses) and returns early, before any interaction/card side effects run. Picking Voice for an agent contact falls through to the normal flow unchanged.
- WHEN the active interaction is an agent-to-agent voice call (`activeInteractionIsAgentCall = OUTBOUND_AGENTS.some(a => a.id === activeInteraction?.id)`, all 3 page files) → the header's own `ChannelStatusTag` status chip is hidden in all 3 tiers (an agent call has no real Open/Pending/Resolved/Closed disposition to log); on Premium/Advanced only (2.0 already hides Customer Information unconditionally via its pre-existing `showCustomerInformation = false`) the docked Customer Information panel wrapper (`showPanelToggle && activeInteraction`) and the floating header toggle/hover-preview block are additionally gated on `!activeInteractionIsAgentCall`, hiding Customer Information outright rather than falling back to the Detail-only tab set an unknown-contact interaction still gets.
- WHEN a New Outbound picker contact has `quickLaunch: true` (lyra-ui's `create-new.tsx`, new optional `CreateNewOutboundContact.quickLaunch` field) → picking ANY channel for that contact skips the "detail" screen (Select Channel/Select Phone/Outbound Skill/Start Interaction form) entirely: `goToDetail` — the internal function both the per-row hover-flyout channel picks and the plain row-click default-channel pick funnel through — checks `contact.quickLaunch` first and, if true, calls `onStartCall` immediately with resolved defaults (first available phone/address via the existing `defaultDetailValueFor` helper; first `skillOptions` entry for skills) and `setOpen(false)`, returning before the `"detail"` screen is ever pushed onto the stack. Customers are unaffected — `quickLaunch` is left unset on them, so they still get the full detail screen.
- In the app's shared `agent-next-gen-outbound-data.tsx` (all 3 tiers pick it up automatically, since it's imported not duplicated) → `quickLaunch: true` is set on every `OUTBOUND_AGENTS` contact (`.map()`) and on both `OUTBOUND_SKILLS` entries (s1/s2); `OUTBOUND_CUSTOMERS`, `OUTBOUND_TEAMS`, and Contact-History-derived outbound contacts are deliberately left unset — per explicit request ("agent or skill", not teams or customers), those still get the full detail screen. `OUTBOUND_TEAM_MEMBERS` inherits `quickLaunch: true` automatically since it reuses the same `OUTBOUND_AGENTS` objects by reference, filtered by team. `OutboundAddButton`/`useOutboundAddButton` (the "+" Add Channel button on an already-open interaction record) was deliberately left untouched — out of scope for this request, which was specific to the New Outbound picker's own contact-row flow.

## 23. Customer record header/tab-row/session-row redesign (Premium/Advanced ONLY, EXCEPT the single "+" trigger/borderless Unassign & Dismiss bullets below, which per explicit follow-up request also apply to 2.0 — see the final bullet; agent-interaction work is a separate future request)

Per explicit request/mockup (4 states: new-outbound draft ×1-channel, new-outbound draft ×2+-channels, active ×1-channel, active ×2+-channels), for EVERY customer interaction on Premium/Advanced (real directory customer or not — supersedes §22's bullet 231):

- `showChannelTabRow` (both page files) is now keyed on CHANNEL COUNT, not customer identity: `!activeInteraction || activeInteraction.threads.length >= 2` (was `!activeInteraction || activeInteractionIsRealCustomer`). `activeInteractionIsRealCustomer` is now a fully independent signal, feeding ONLY Customer Information's own tab-set/customer-matching UI (§21/§235's flows, unchanged) — no longer wired into the tab row at all. Because every downstream surface already keyed off `showChannelTabRow` (the header's own action cluster, `showSessionActionCluster={showChannelTabRow}` at the `<InteractionTranscript>` call site, the `!showChannelTabRow` subtitle branch), this one redefinition cascades automatically: with exactly 1 channel open, the tab row hides and the header's icon-button cluster (Consult/Transfer, Outcome, kebab, status chip, Unassign & Dismiss-or-Delete-Draft) takes over; the moment a 2nd channel opens, the real `TabList` comes back and those same actions relocate down to `TranscriptSessionSeparator`'s own session-row cluster, scoped to whichever session is current.
- The `PageHeader`'s `subtitle` (both page files) is now UNCONDITIONAL — no longer gated behind `!showChannelTabRow` — so it reads the active channel's own "{icon} {Channel label} | {Draft/date}" pattern (`CHANNEL_TYPE_META`/`activeChannelDateTime`) regardless of channel count, falling back to the plain customer ID only when there's no active channel at all. Per the mockup, even the 2-channel/tab-row states keep this pattern (e.g. "Email | Draft") rather than reverting to the plain customer ID the moment a tab row appears.
- Bug fix, header cluster (both page files): the header's `ChannelStatusTag` + its trailing vertical `Separator` are now ALSO gated behind `!activeChannelIsNewOutboundThread` (previously only excluded the agent-call case) — a genuine, never-launched draft thread now correctly shows NOTHING in that slot (matching the mockup's 1-channel-draft state: just the red Delete Draft trash icon, no status chip) instead of the chip/separator rendering alone once every other icon in the cluster had already collapsed.
- Bug fix, session row (`TranscriptSessionSeparator`, agent-next-gen-transcript.tsx, shared): its own `ChannelStatusTag` is now ALSO gated behind `!isNewThread` (previously rendered regardless) — so the 2+-channel/draft state's session row shows ONLY the red Delete Draft trash icon, matching the header's equivalent fix above and the mockup's own 2-channel-draft state (trash icon only, nothing else).
- Kebab consolidation ("Kebab Menu" mockup callout), 3 render sites: the header cluster in BOTH page files, and (newly) `TranscriptSessionSeparator`'s own session-row cluster — the 3 separate, permanently-visible Send Transcript/Download Transcript/Translate Messages `ActionIconButton`s are replaced by a single kebab (⋮) `KebabMenuButton` ("More Options") whose dropdown holds those same 3 decorative, still-unwired items. Gated the same as the rest of that cluster in each location (`!activeChannelIsNewOutboundThread` in the header, `!isClosed && !isNewThread` in the session row, matching Consult/Transfer's own gate there).
- Add Channel trigger, superseded (both page files, per explicit follow-up request): the per-channel icon row in `titleSuffix` and the custom ad-hoc-only `AddChannelAdHocButton` popup (both described in the two bullets this replaces, prior revision) are GONE — `titleSuffix` is no longer used on this `PageHeader` at all. In their place, a single "+" trigger sits in `actions`, immediately LEFT of the "Customer Information" button: the plain, un-locked `getHeaderAction(activeInteraction.id, className, { label: "Add Channel", showLabel: false })` call (no `initialChannel`), which opens lyra-ui's own stock `OutboundAddButton` popup — Select Channel radios (Voice/Email/WhatsApp), Select Phone/Outbound Skill, "Start Interaction" — same call this used for its OLD narrow-width (`recordHeaderWidth < 768`) `titleSuffix` fallback, now used unconditionally regardless of header width and regardless of `showChannelTabRow`/channel count (always rendered, like Customer Information beside it). `AddChannelAdHocButton`'s own file (`agent-next-gen-add-channel-button.tsx`) and each page's `handleAddAdHocChannel` handler were deleted outright — no longer referenced anywhere. `getAvailableChannels` (from `useOutboundAddButton`) is likewise no longer destructured in either page file, only `getHeaderAction`.
- Unassign & Dismiss border removed (header cluster, both page files, per explicit request): the far-right `Button` switched from `variant="outline"` (with `border-lyra-status-critical-strong`/`hover:border-lyra-status-critical-strong` classes) to `variant="ghost"` with no border classes at all — same critical-red icon/text color and `hover:bg-lyra-status-critical-subtle`/`active:bg-lyra-status-critical-medium` background states, just borderless now.
- Per a later explicit follow-up request ("use a ghost button error variant for the unassign and dismiss in the session row"), the session row's own separate copy of this button (`TranscriptSessionSeparator`, agent-next-gen-transcript.tsx — shared across all 3 tiers) gets the identical `outline` → `ghost` treatment: same border classes dropped, same critical-red color/hover/active states kept. No named `ghost`+`critical`/error `Button` variant exists (button.tsx's own `variant` list is `default`/`destructive`/`warning`/`success`/`outline`/`ghost`/`icon`), so this composes `ghost` with the same hand-picked critical-token classes the header cluster's own copy already established, rather than inventing a new named variant for a single button.
- 2.0 now gets the Unassign & Dismiss border-removal change above (AgentNextGenPage.tsx): the header's own Unassign & Dismiss `Button` gets the identical `variant="outline"` → `variant="ghost"`/border-classes-removed treatment described above.
- 2.0's own "+" Add Channel trigger (AgentNextGenPage.tsx), per explicit follow-up request extending this section's scope: `showAddChannelActions` (previously hardcoded `false` — Add Channel was hidden outright in 2.0's header per §22's own record of that decision) is now `true`, and its render site no longer branches on `recordHeaderWidth`/per-channel icons at all. Per a further explicit follow-up request (screenshot: the button was silently missing for an ad-hoc/unknown-contact interaction), this does NOT use the plain `getHeaderAction` call Premium/Advanced's own "+" trigger uses (bullet above) — that call looks up the interaction's own id in `outboundConfig.groups` (the real customer/agent directory) and returns `null` (no button at all) for any id that isn't a known contact, a real, already-shipped bug `handleRedial`'s own doc comment documents in detail. Since 2.0 has no real customer database by design — every interaction here is 1:1, frequently a raw quick-dialed number or other ad-hoc contact with no directory record — that lookup fails often enough to matter. Instead, 2.0 restores `AddChannelAdHocButton` (`agent-next-gen-add-channel-button.tsx`, previously deleted as part of Premium/Advanced's own Phase D cleanup, now reinstated specifically for this file): a plain "Enter Email Or Phone Number" `Input` inside a small "Add Channel" `Popover` — no directory/contact search, no Select Channel/Phone/Skill picker — auto-detects email vs. phone and, once valid, a "Continue with '{value}'" button launches immediately. `onLaunch(query, channel)` feeds a page-local `handleAddAdHocChannel` handler (mirrors Premium/Advanced's own pre-Phase-D handler of the same name) that builds a minimal `Thread` and appends it directly onto the CURRENTLY ACTIVE interaction (by `activeInteraction.id`, not contact id) — deliberately not routed through `handleStartCall`, and sets `currentThreadId` to the new thread so it opens as the active tab immediately. `getAvailableChannels` is no longer destructured from `useOutboundAddButton` in this file (only `getHeaderAction`, still used elsewhere for the LeftNav card's own "+").
  - Trigger, per follow-up request ("that button is not standard" / "the plus button is displaying a black plus"): NOT `ActionIconButton` (its hardcoded `variant="icon"` baked in `text-lyra-fg-action`, and a `className` color override to `text-lyra-fg-on-primary` didn't reliably win — `cn()`'s tailwind-merge doesn't recognize those two custom lyra-* color-token classes as the same conflicting utility, so cascade order decided the winner, rendering a black "+" instead of white-on-blue). Now a plain `Button variant="default" size="icon-md"` — `buttonVariants`' own `default` variant already bakes in the correct solid-primary background/white-text/`rounded-lyra-sm` combo with zero overrides needed, matching every other button's shape in this app.
  - Popover, per follow-up request ("the padding on the popover is not correct" / "the padding-bottom is incorrect"): width `w-80` → `w-96` and placeholder shortened from the full "name@example.com or (555) 555-5555" to "Email or phone number" — the longer placeholder didn't actually fit `Popover`'s own default `px-5` body inset at the narrower width, clipping against the input's edge (read as "no padding" even though the real horizontal inset was always applied correctly). The "Continue with" button was moved OUT of `content` (where it shared that div's own `pb-2`) and into `Popover`'s real `footer` prop (`px-5 pb-4 pt-1`, matching every other footer action row in this app, e.g. `agent-next-gen-transcript.tsx`'s Close/Cancel and Approve & Save rows) — this exact hand-rolled-footer-inside-content shape is the anti-pattern CLAUDE.md rule #28 already documents and fixed once before elsewhere in this codebase.
  - Email/phone detection, per explicit request ("why did you not use the lyra-ui components properly?"): the component's own `looksLikeAdHocEmail`/`looksLikeAdHocPhone` were near-verbatim re-derivations of lyra-ui's own (previously-private) `looksLikeEmail`/`looksLikePhoneNumber` in `create-new.tsx` — a direct violation of CLAUDE.md's Rule zero ("never modify a lyra-ui core component from here... not approximate it locally"). Fixed at the source: both functions are now `export`ed from `create-new.tsx` and re-exported from lyra-ui's `index.ts`; this component imports and calls the real ones, and its own duplicated copies are deleted outright.
  - Position, per explicit follow-up request ("the '+' should go to the left of the separator not the right of the trash can"): moved from AFTER the header's action cluster closed (rendering to the right of Unassign & Dismiss/Delete Draft) to INSIDE that cluster, immediately before the vertical `Separator` that sets the trash/dismiss button apart. Visual order is now status chip → "+" Add Channel → divider → trash/Unassign & Dismiss.
  - Popover bottom padding, per explicit follow-up request ("update the padding below the enter email or phone input to match the padding-left and right"): `content`'s own bottom padding is now `pb-5` (20px, matching the `px-5` horizontal inset `Popover`'s `bodyPadding` already applies) instead of `pb-2` (8px) — but only when the "Continue with" footer ISN'T showing (`!canContinue`); when it is, `content` keeps the tighter `pb-2` since the footer's own `pt-1` immediately follows it and supplies the real bottom edge itself (`px-5 pb-4 pt-1`), so both together don't double up.
  - Opens as a new tab, per explicit follow-up request ("when the channel is added it should open a new tab" / "open a tab when a new channel is added" — flagged three times before this fix landed): 2.0's own `showChannelTabRow`/`showSessionActionCluster` were BOTH hardcoded (`false`) on the premise that 2.0 interactions are always exactly 1:1 with never more than one thread — an assumption `AddChannelAdHocButton` itself broke the moment it started letting an agent add a 2nd thread onto the active interaction. `handleAddAdHocChannel` was already correctly setting `currentThreadId` to the new thread, but with `showChannelTabRow` pinned `false` there was no tab row left to actually show it — the new channel silently replaced the content pane with no way back to the first one. Fixed by deriving BOTH constants exactly the way Premium/Advanced already do (`showChannelTabRow = !activeInteraction || activeInteraction.threads.length >= 2`; `showSessionActionCluster={showChannelTabRow}` at the `InteractionTranscript` call site, no longer its own separate constant) — the real `TabList`/`ChannelTab` strip below the record header (already fully built, previously unreachable in this file since `showChannelTabRow` could never be `true`) now appears automatically the moment a 2nd thread exists, and the header's own per-channel icon cluster (already gated `!showChannelTabRow && activeChannel`) correctly hands off to each session row's own copy of that cluster, same cascade as Premium/Advanced.
  - Stays visible after adding a channel, per explicit follow-up request ("don't remove the add channel '+' from the header after a channel is added - the agents can add as many emails/phones as they want to the interaction"): the "+" trigger's render call was pulled OUT of the `!showChannelTabRow && activeChannel` cluster it used to sit inside (gated only on `showAddChannelActions && activeChannel` now) — previously it disappeared the instant a 2nd thread existed and that whole cluster relocated down to the session row (the "opens as a new tab" fix just above), capping an agent at exactly one extra ad-hoc channel before losing the means to add a third. `handleAddAdHocChannel` always operates on `activeInteraction` regardless of existing thread count, so there was never an actual limit — only the button's own visibility was wrongly tied to `showChannelTabRow`. Single-thread visual order is unchanged (status chip → "+" → divider → trash/Unassign & Dismiss); once a 2nd+ thread exists, that whole gated cluster (including the divider and trash/dismiss button) hands off to the session row as before, but "+" now keeps rendering on its own.
- 2.0's header `ChannelStatusTag`, per explicit follow-up request: now ALSO gated behind `!activeChannelIsNewOutboundThread` (previously only excluded the agent-call case, `!activeInteractionIsAgentCall`) — matching the identical bug fix already applied to Premium/Advanced's own copy of this chip (bullet above). A freshly-launched, agent-initiated outbound draft (`activeInteraction.startedFresh` true, `activeChannel.lastCustomerMessageTick` still undefined) no longer shows "Open" the instant the agent sends the first message — the chip stays hidden until the CUSTOMER actually replies, not just once the thread is launched.
- "+" Add Channel fallback for unknown interactions (Premium/Advanced), per explicit follow-up request ("add the '+' to the unknown interactions in premium and advanced"): `getHeaderAction(activeInteraction.id, ...)` (bullet above) returns `null` — no button at all — for any interaction id not registered in `contactsById` (the combined `outboundConfig.groups` + `contact-history` synthetic-id group, both page files' own `useOutboundAddButton` call). A genuinely ad-hoc/unknown interaction (no real `CREATE_NEW_CUSTOMERS` record, never routed through `handleRedial`/`handleReopenContactHistoryEntry`'s own synthetic-id registration) hits exactly this gap — same directory-lookup bug 2.0 already has a fix for. Both page files now fall back to `AddChannelAdHocButton` (`?? (<AddChannelAdHocButton onLaunch={handleAddAdHocChannel} .../>)`) whenever `getHeaderAction` returns `null` — the identical directory-independent "Enter Email Or Phone Number" popup 2.0 uses for every one of its own interactions. Each page file gained its own `handleAddAdHocChannel` handler (recreated from 2.0's copy, inserted right after `handleRedial`) — both were deleted from these two files during Phase D, since Premium/Advanced switched to the stock picker for known customers; known customers still get that richer picker exactly as before, only genuinely unknown ones fall through to the ad-hoc field.
- Every OTHER bullet in this section (tab-row/subtitle/kebab consolidation behavior) remains Premium/Advanced-only; 2.0's own subtitle logic (§22) is untouched, and its header kebab (Send/Download/Translate) is still 3 separate icons, not consolidated — that consolidation was never extended to this file. (2.0's own tab row is no longer PERMANENTLY hidden — see "Opens as a new tab" above — but it's still off by default for every single-thread interaction, same as Premium/Advanced.)
- Tab close icon unified to a trash can, per explicit follow-up request ("the 'X' close icons in the tabs should be trash cans to match the other draft tab structure") — a `lyra-ui` core change (`tabs.tsx`'s `Tab` component), so it applies everywhere a tab's `onRemove` renders across all 3 tiers, not just 2.0. `removeVariant` ("close" vs. "delete-draft") used to pick BOTH the icon (plain "×" vs. `Trash2`) and the color (this tab's own active/inactive color vs. always-red); it now only controls the color — every `onRemove` button renders `Trash2` regardless of variant. A closed channel/interaction's tab (previously a plain "×") and a never-launched draft's tab (already `Trash2`, red) now show the same icon, just differently colored.
  - Color AND size unified too, per a later explicit follow-up request ("make all the delete trash icons in the tabs the same size and color as the tabs themselves (not red)") — same `tabs.tsx` `Tab` component. `removeVariant === "delete-draft"`'s always-red color is gone entirely; every `onRemove` button now reads this tab's own plain active/inactive color, the exact same fallback its leading `icon` slot already uses (`active ? activeTextClass : "text-lyra-fg-disabled group-hover:text-lyra-fg-secondary"`). The `Trash2` glyph itself grew from a fixed `width={11} height={11}` to `h-4 w-4` (16px), matching every real `ChannelTab` leading icon's own size exactly (chat/email/SMS/WhatsApp/voice are all `h-4 w-4`, channel-row.tsx) — its wrapping button grew from `h-4 w-4` to `h-5 w-5` to keep a couple pixels of padding around the now-larger icon, matching the `menuItems` kebab slot's own `h-5 w-5` wrapper (the same trailing position's mutually-exclusive alternate). `removeVariant` itself still exists as a prop, but only feeds `removeLabel`'s own accessible text ("Delete Draft" vs. "Close {label}") now — no remaining visual effect in `Tab`.

## 24. Auto "Working" agent status while on a live voice call (all 3 tiers)

Per explicit request ("when an agent is in a call the status should change to 'working' — do not add this as a selectable status — and the badge should be a warning with a dot"):

- `"working"` is a new member of lyra-ui's own `AgentStatus` union (`agent-profile.tsx`), with a `statusConfig` entry (`label: "Working"`, `textColor`/`color: text-lyra-status-warning-strong`/`bg-lyra-status-warning-strong`, `badgeVariant: "warning"`, deliberately NO `icon`) — but it's deliberately left OUT of that component's own `allStatuses` array, the literal list its status-search/Favorites/"All Codes" menu renders. That's the entire mechanism keeping it unselectable — an agent can never pick "Working" from the dropdown; only a consumer's own `status`/`onStatusChange` (below) ever sets it.
- Bug fix, `StatusIcon` (agent-profile.tsx, shared by the avatar's own corner badge and every status-menu row's leading icon): the no-`icon` fallback path used to hardcode `variant="neutral"` regardless of that status's own `badgeVariant`, since the only status ever reaching that branch before "working" happened to have neither set. Now reads `badgeVariant ?? "neutral"`, so "working" — which sets `badgeVariant: "warning"` with no `icon` — correctly renders a plain WARNING-colored dot (orange, per the request) instead of falling back to gray.
- Each page file (`AgentNextGenPage.tsx`, `AgentWorkspace2WithDeskPage.tsx`, `AgentWorkspaceAdvancedPage.tsx`) gained an `isOnVoiceCall` derivation — `interactions.some(i => !i.closed && i.threads.some(t => t.type === "voice" && i.threadStatuses?.[t.id] !== "Closed"))` — true for ANY still-open interaction with a live voice thread, not just whichever interaction is currently being viewed, so switching over to check a different customer's chat mid-call doesn't wrongly clear "Working." A `useEffect` keyed only on `isOnVoiceCall` (not `agentStatus` — this is edge-triggered, reacting to the call actually starting/ending, not re-firing for unrelated status changes) sets `agentStatus` to `"working"` the moment it flips true (capturing whatever status was active into a `preCallStatusRef` first), and restores that captured status (falling back to `"unavailable"` if somehow unset) the moment it flips back to false. `setElapsedSeconds(0)` on both transitions, matching every other status change's own timer-reset behavior (`handleStatusChange`).
- `AgentProfile`'s own trigger button (`name`/`timer` shown next to the avatar) needs no changes — it already reads `statusConfig[status].label`/`.textColor` generically, so "Working (00:03)" and its warning coloring fall out of the `statusConfig` entry above for free.

## 25. Channel controls always live in the session row (all 3 tiers) — supersedes §23 bullet 253's relocation cascade

Per explicit follow-up request ("let's update the channel controls to always be in the session row - even if there is only one channel open (no tabs) - moving them around is confusing"): the record-header icon-button cluster (Consult/Transfer, Outcome, kebab "More Options", status chip, Unassign & Dismiss/Delete Draft) that used to relocate INTO the header whenever `showChannelTabRow` read `false` (exactly 1 channel open) is gone from all 3 page files' record headers outright — it now lives ONLY in `TranscriptSessionSeparator`'s own session-row cluster (agent-next-gen-transcript.tsx, shared), unconditionally, regardless of channel count.

- All 3 page files: the `!showChannelTabRow && activeChannel && (...)` block that used to render Consult/Transfer, the Outcome `Popover`, the Send/Download/Translate kebab, and the `ChannelStatusTag` status chip inside the record header's `actions` was deleted outright (replaced with a short doc comment pointing at the session row). The second `!showChannelTabRow && activeChannel && (...)` block right after the "+" Add Channel button — the Delete-Draft-trash-icon-or-Unassign-&-Dismiss-button toggle, on Premium/Advanced preceded by a vertical `Separator` — was also deleted outright. The record header's `actions` now only ever renders the "+" Add Channel trigger and the Customer Information toggle/hover-preview.
- All 3 page files: `showSessionActionCluster={showChannelTabRow}` at the `<InteractionTranscript>` call site is now just `showSessionActionCluster` (no value — always `true`), relying on that prop's own default (`InteractionTranscript`/`TranscriptSessionSeparator` both already default it to `true` in their own signatures). `showChannelTabRow` itself is UNCHANGED — it still governs only whether the real `TabList`/`ChannelTab` tab row renders below the record header; it no longer has any bearing on where this action cluster lives.
- `TranscriptSessionSeparator`'s own internal gating (`!isClosed`, `!isNewThread` for Consult/Transfer/Outcome/kebab, `onDismiss` only ever passed for the CURRENT session) is unchanged and already correctly limits the cluster to the live/current session row only, never a stale historical one — so flipping this prop to always-`true` at the page level required no changes inside `TranscriptSessionSeparator` itself.
- Dead code removed from each of the 3 page files as a result: `headerOutcomePopoverState` (`useOutcomePopoverState()`), the header's own `headerStatusMenuOpen`/`headerStatusMenuView` state and its 4 handlers (`handleHeaderStatusMenuOpenChange`/`handleHeaderSelectStatus`/`handleHeaderConfirmCloseStatus`/`handleHeaderCancelCloseStatus`), and the now-unused `buildOutcomePopoverSlots`/`ConsultTransferIcon`/`ChannelStatusTag` imports plus the `UserX`/`Send`/`FileDown`/`Languages`/`CircleCheck`/`Trash2` lucide-react icon imports (each icon is still used inside `agent-next-gen-transcript.tsx`'s own session-row copy, just no longer imported redundantly into the page files). `AgentNextGenPage.tsx` additionally dropped its now-unused `activeInteractionIsAgentCall` derivation, which only ever fed that same removed header status chip.
- Net effect: an interaction with only 1 channel open no longer shows a special "compact" header with its own copy of these controls — the header is now just title/subtitle/"+"/Customer Information for every interaction, real customer or not, 1 channel or many, and the session row (already the single home for these controls once 2+ channels were open) is now the single home for them period.

## 26. Session row max-width (shared, all 3 tiers) — currently FULL WIDTH (flipped twice)

Per a THIRD explicit follow-up request ("dang - revert that last change - go back to full screen width"), un-reverting the immediately preceding revert below: back to full width again. `InteractionTranscript`'s outer per-session scroll wrapper (agent-next-gen-transcript.tsx) is `w-full` with no max-width, `TranscriptSessionSeparator`'s own root carries its own `px-6` directly again (not inherited from a constrained parent), and the per-session message-content wrapper carries its own `max-w-[1200px] mx-auto px-6` again, around just the message bubbles — i.e. exactly the shape the ORIGINAL change (two bullets below) first established. Verified tsc/eslint-clean (70-error/0-eslint-error baseline unchanged).
